HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000443/B001","root_000787/B001","root_001651/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1239aaa5f3e0fb97ffc3","text":"1. **Evil as a retreating whisper-process.** Evidence: ش ر ر B001, evil and badness; و س و س B001/B003, hidden inward whisper and Waswas as Satan’s name; خ ن س B001, withdrawal into concealment or delay. Structural cue: this ayah finally names the object of refuge after the three divine titles. Assigned role: شر is not an abstract property but the harm produced by an agent/process that whispers and withdraws. Abductive move: the sequence delays the enemy’s naming so the reader first sees the authority structure under which the enemy is contained. Reading change: “evil” reads as a mode of covert action with a retreat-cycle, not merely a moral category.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B003","root_001651/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_183562bb2b35a7e4170e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:4:2\",\"root_000787\",[\"B003\"]],[\"114:4:3\",\"root_001651\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Fısıltının zararı, küçük bir başlangıcın iç dünyada büyük bir etkiye dönüşebilmesi bakımından kıvılcım gibi işleyebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B002","root_001651/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1f484a058338dde0508b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:4:2\",\"root_000787\",[\"B002\"]],[\"114:4:3\",\"root_001651\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gizli telkin, insanlara ait söz dolaşımına katıldığında bireysel iç konuşmadan toplumsal olarak yayılan kötülüğe dönüşebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000443/B001","root_000787/B009","root_001651/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_40afbd5c4ad2d963c1fd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:4:2\",\"root_000787\",[\"B009\"]],[\"114:4:3\",\"root_001651\",[\"B002\"]],[\"114:4:4\",\"root_000443\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Zarar, açık bir darbeden çok, küçük fakat sürekli temaslarla dikkati örten ve zamanla biriken bir rahatsızlık biçiminde de işleyebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_55810eba6f602bc6bee3","text":"114:5 specified what this ayah had only compressed: the evil of the whisperer is located in the chests, so ش ر ر B003 as sparks and و س و س B002 as faint murmur became stronger than a generic evil-reading. 114:6 then prevented a single-source reading: the whisperer’s evil may pass through concealed agencies and through people. Earlier ayat also changed this verse: the authority triad in 114:1-3 means the enemy is named only after the protective hierarchy has been established. The surprise is that the ayah’s threat is both tiny and institutionally contained: a spark-like, retreating murmur named under Lord/King/God.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B002","root_001651/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_57357f258be09c4bcc7e","text":"4. **Spread and circulation of evil-talk.** Evidence: ش ر ر B002, spreading something out to dry; ق و ل B007 from 114:1, circulating talk/public report; س م و B008 from 114:0, good repute spread; و س و س B001, hidden inward whisper. Structural cue: the packet contrasts raised good naming at the opening with hidden whisper later, both within a repeated الناس field. Assigned role: شر can be laid out and distributed as talk across a social surface. Abductive move: inward whisper becomes socially effective when it is spread through circulating speech among people. Reading change: the evil is both internal and social: a hidden suggestion can dry out into public report.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000443/B001","root_000787/B001","root_001651/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_66ff0fe42975fa9becb8","text":"1. **The evil is a self-concealing cycle.** و س و س `B003` permits `ٱلْوَسْوَاسِ` as the named whispering agent, while `B001` supplies hidden inner talk; خ ن س `B001` supplies contraction, delay, and withdrawal into concealment. The noun in this ayah becomes the verb `يُوَسْوِسُ` in 114:5, so agent, act, and retreat are sequentially joined. The packet supplies whispering plus withdrawal; I infer approach -> implanting a thought -> retreat -> renewed approach. ش ر ر `B001` then names not just a bad content but the harm of a recurrent operation that evades sustained inspection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B009","root_001651/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_70f6026a10add83a91be","text":"4. **Harm may work by low-intensity recurrence rather than open attack.** The dormant ش ر ر `B009` image is a gnat-like nuisance that covers a person's face without biting. It is structurally sharpened by و س و س `B002` faint sound and خ ن س `B001` disappearance: all three images avoid a single overt blow. I infer repeated minor contact -> accumulated distraction or aversion; that accumulation is my arrow, not an explicit packet claim. `مِن شَرِّ` thus covers subtle, ambient harassment alongside morally bad content.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001651/B001","root_001651/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_737812d5ab416f06c867","text":"3. **The whisper can masquerade as one's own inner saying.** و س و س `B001` is inward self-talk and `B002` a barely audible rustle or murmur; contextual ق و ل `B012` is an unexpressed saying conceived within the self. The next ayah places the process inside `صُدُورِ`. The packet supplies overlapping hidden-speech images; I infer overlap of channel -> uncertainty about authorship, not identity of source. `شَرِّ ٱلْوَسْوَاسِ` consequently includes the danger that an alien prompt is experienced as self-generated thought.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001651/B001","root_001651/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7f0f7c72066af3c52af7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:4:3\",\"root_001651\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Son ayet, aynı gizli fısıldama işleminin hem görünmeyen hem de insan kaynaklı faillerce taşınabileceğini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001651/B001","root_001651/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7f17388780810b8d5d36","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:4:3\",\"root_001651\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İçte ve belli belirsiz işleyen fısıltı, yabancı bir telkinin kişinin kendi düşüncesiymiş gibi algılanmasına yol açabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B009","root_001651/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_811e37c0d3625f534315","text":"3. **Dispersed nuisance rather than frontal assault.** Evidence: ش ر ر B009, gnat-like nuisance covering the human face without biting; و س و س B002, faint sound; ن و س B001 from repeated الناس, swaying/dangling susceptibility. Structural cue: the named enemy is “وسواس” and “خناس,” both suggesting indirectness, faintness, and withdrawal. Assigned role: the threat harasses perception and attention rather than striking openly. Abductive move: because the harm is described by hidden sound plus retreat, its force may be irritation, distraction, and coverage. Reading change: the ayah reads as protection from a low-grade but pervasive affliction, not only from an overt attacker.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000443/B001","root_000443/B002","root_000443/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b2c4afe1164e39f45fbc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:4:4\",\"root_000443\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Fısıldayanın hareketi yalnız geri çekilme değil, doğrultudan sapıp yeniden dönmedir; و س و س B002, hidden rustle or murmur; ص د ر B001 from 114:5, chest; ج ن ن B010 from 114:6, hidden heart or inner fear. Structural cue: 114:5 immediately locates the whispering “in the breasts of people.” Assigned role: sparks model the small, mobile, dangerous particles of whisper entering the interior. Abductive move: a faint whisper can be causally like a spark: tiny at contact, large in possible effect. Reading change: “شر” becomes a subtle ignition in the inner person, not only a named evil to flee.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d1b26963f7be7a09a687","text":"114:5's shift from the noun `ٱلْوَسْوَاسِ` to the verb `يُوَسْوِسُ`, now located in breasts, made the approach-withdraw cycle more than a lexical possibility. 114:6 then corrected an overly singular source-reading: the same covert operation can be carried by human agents and circulate socially.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000443/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_de303859bbbfc5068ce7","text":"2. **`ٱلْخَنَّاسِ` exposes a weakness as well as a tactic.** The scope of خ ن س `B001` explicitly includes the devil withdrawing at remembrance. Earlier ayat supply the basmalah, ق و ل `B001` overt utterance, ع و ذ `B002` protective recitation, and the three divine titles. The packet supplies withdrawal-at-remembrance and a voiced invocation; I infer the invocation -> forcing the hidden agent to recede. The adjective therefore reads in two directions at once: concealment makes the whisperer elusive, but retreat also makes it interruptible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000443/B001","root_000443/B002","root_000443/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e4df9d7212be16fe76c2","text":"5. **Veering and retrograde motion.** Evidence: خ ن س B001, withdrawing/hiding/delaying; خ ن س B002, stars or planets that hide and retreat; خ ن س B005, horse veering right and left while running straight; ن و س B001, swaying oscillation. Structural cue: الخناس stands at the end of the ayah as a descriptive epithet after الوسواس. Assigned role: خناس supplies the motion-pattern: approach, retreat, veer, and return. Abductive move: the whisperer’s danger is rhythmic and evasive; it cannot be read as a single static presence. Reading change: the ayah becomes a description of an intermittent, elusive enemy whose motion exploits human oscillation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
